rI(re,rH,rn,rO,rT)re[rH]=((re[rH]+re[rn]))%ER re[rT]=Eu(Es(re[rT],re[rH]),16)re[rO]=((re[rO]+re[rT]))%ER re[rn]=Eu(Es(re[rn],re[rO]),12)re[rH]=((re[rH]+re[rn]))%ER re[rT]=Eu(Es(re[rT],re[rH]),8)re[rO]=((re[rO]+re[rT]))%ER re[rn]=Eu(Es(re[rn],re[rO]),7)end local function rx(rt,rA,ra,rQ,rX)local rw={1634760805;857760878;2036477234;1797285236}for rY=1,8,1 do rw[rY+4]=rt[rY]end rw[13],rw[14],rw[15],rw[16]=rA,ra,rQ,rX local rV={}for rD=1,16,1 do rV[rD]=rw[rD]end for rp=1,10,1 do rI(rV,1,5,9,13)rI(rV,2,6,10,14)rI(rV,3,7,11,15)rI(rV,4,8,12,16)rI(rV,1,6,11,16)rI(rV,2,7,12,13)rI(rV,3,8,9,14)rI(rV,4,5,10,15)end for rk=1,16,1 do rV[rk]=((rV[rk]+rw[rk]))%ER end return rV end local function rj(rf,rC,rN,rg,ro,rS)if#rf==0 then return rf end rN,rg,ro,rS=rN or 1,rg or 0,ro or 0,rS or 0 local rb,rW={},1 while rW<=#rf do local rR=rx(rC,rN,rg,ro,rS)for rU=1,16,1 do local rs=rR[rU]for ru=1,4,1 do if rW>#rf then break end rb[rW]=Eb(Es(ES(rf,rW),rs%256))rs,rW=Eo(rs/256),rW+1 end end rN=((rN+1))%ER end return EW(rb)end local function rm(rL,rF,rM)return rx(rL,0,rF,rM%ER,Eo(rM/ER))end local function rh(rJ,rz,ri,KB,KG)local Kq=rm(KG,ri,KB)local Kc={Es(rz,Kq[1])}for KE=1,8,1 do Kc[KE+1]=Es(rJ[KE],Kq[KE+1])end Kc[10]=Kq[10]return Kc end local function Kr(KK,Ky,Kd,Kv)local Kl=rm(Kv,Ky,Kd)return{Es(KK,Kl[1]);Kl[10]}end local function KZ(KP,KI,Ke,KH,Kn)local KO=rm(Kn,Ke,KH)return KP[2]==KO[10]and KP[1]==Es(KI,KO[1])end local function KT(Kx,Kt,KA,Ka,KQ)local KX=rm(KQ,KA,Ka)if Kx[10]~=KX[10]or Kx[1]~=Es(Kt,KX[1])then return nil end local Kw={}for KY=1,8,1 do Kw[KY]=Es(Kx[KY+1],KX[KY+1])end return Kw end local function KV(KD)local Kp={}for Kk=1,#KD,1 do local Kj=KD[Kk]Kp[Kk]=Eb(Kj%256,Eo(Kj/256)%256,Eo(Kj/65536)%256,Eo(Kj/16777216)%256)end return EW(Kp)end return{block=rx;crypt=rj,seal=rh;sealAdmission=Kr;admit=KZ;resolve=KT;packWords=KV}end))()local KC={2448897055,1791961284;2233262848,998503748,311049257,1255299074,3040725261;3230617380}local function KN(Kg,Ko,KS)local Kb=(Bx(21161448))local KW={}local KR=Bt[Bx(1861049490)][Bx(1111672996)]for KU=1,#Kb,1 do KW[KR(Kb,KU)]=KU-1 end local Ks=Bt[Bx(1861049490)][Bx(435154284)]local Ku=Bt[Bx(206067799)][Bx(869715394)]local Km=Bt[Bx(1861049490)][Bx(749388225)]local KL=Bt[Bx(1978196077)][Bx(1729955098)]local KF=Bt[Bx(1978196077)][Bx(1918676890)]local function KM(Kh)return Kf[Bx(544224030)](Kh,Ko,KS)end if Bt[Bx(736152160)](Kg)~=(Bx(1861049490))then return Kg end local KJ=Ks(Kg)local Kz={}local Ki=1 while Ki<=KJ do local yB=(KJ-Ki)+1 local yG=yB>=5 and 5 or yB local yq=0 local yc=yG>1 for yE=0,4,1 do local yr if yE<yG then yr=KW[KR(Kg,Ki+yE)]if not yr then yc=false break end else yr=84 end yq=yq*85+yr end if yc then local yK=Ku(yq/16777216)%256 local yy=Ku(yq/65536)%256 local yd=Ku(yq/256)%256 local yv=yq%256 if yG==5 then KL(Kz,Km(yK,yy,yd,yv))elseif yG==4 then KL(Kz,Km(yK,yy,yd))elseif yG==3 then KL(Kz,Km(yK,yy))elseif yG==2 then KL(Kz,Km(yK))end end Ki=Ki+yG end return KM(KF(Kz))end local yl={}local yS=((function(yZ,yP,yI,ye)local yH=KN(yI,ye)local yn,yO=Bt[Bx(1861049490)][Bx(1111672996)],Bt[Bx(1861049490)][Bx(980351290)]local function yT(yx,yt)local yA,ya,yQ,yX=yn(yx,yt,yt+3)return((yA+ya*256)+yQ*65536)+yX*16777216 end local yw={}local yY,yV=1,1 for yD=1,#yH,4 do local yp=yT(yH,yD)local yk=yp%65536 local yj=Bt[Bx(206067799)][Bx(869715394)](yp/65536)yw[yk]={yY,yj,yV}yY,yV=yY+yj,yV+1 end yH=nil return function(yf)local yC=yw[yf]if not yC then return nil end local yN=yO(yZ,yC[1],(yC[1]+yC[2])-1)yN=KN(yN,yP,yC[3])if#yN~=8 and#yN~=40 then return nil end local yg={}for yo=1,#yN/4,1 do yg[yo]=yT(yN,((yo-1))*4+1)end return yg end
